[page 001]
                     Abhidhammapiṭake paṭṭhānaṃ
                       chaṭṭhamo bhāgo
                       paccanīyapaṭṭhānaṃ
                      ------------
             namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa
                      paccanīyatikapaṭṭhānaṃ
                        nakusalattikaṃ
      [1] Nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: akusalaṃ abyākataṃ ekaṃ khandhaṃ paṭicca akusalo abyākato
 tayo khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañca rūpaṃ dve khandhe paṭicca dve
 khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañca rūpaṃ. Nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo ca
 nābyākato ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nakusalo ca nākusalo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:. Pañca.
      [2] Nākusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nākusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nākusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato dhammo uppajjati
-------------------------------------------------
[page 002]
 Hetupaccayā: nākusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo ca nābyākato ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nākusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo ca
 nākusalo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca.
       [3] Nābyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nābyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nābyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nābyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo ca nābyākato ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nābyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nākusalo ca nābyākato ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 nābyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo ca nākusalo ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: cha.
       [4] Nakusalañca nābyākatañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalañca nābyākatañca dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalañca nābyākatañca
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nakusalañca nābyākatañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo ca nābyākato ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nakusalañca nābyākatañca dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nakusalo ca nākusalo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 pañca.
       [5] Nākusalañca nābyākatañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo dhammo
-------------------------------------------------
[page 003]
 Uppajjati hetupaccayā: nākusalañca nābyākatañca dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nākusalañca
 nābyākatañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nākusalañca nābyākatañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo ca
 nābyākato ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nākusalañca
 nābyākatañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo ca nākusalo ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca.
       [6] Nakusalañca nākusalañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalañca nākusalañca dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalañca nākusalañca
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo ca nākusalo ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
       [7] Nākusalaṃ 1- dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo 2- dhammo uppajjati
 ārammaṇapaccayā:. [3]-
       [8] Hetuyā ekūnattiṃsa ārammaṇe catuvīsa avigate
 ekūnattiṃsa.
             Sahajātavārampi paccayavārampi nissayavārampi
             saṃsaṭṭhavārampi sampayuttavārampi paṭiccavārasadisaṃ.
       [9] Nakusalo dhammo nakusalassa dhammassa hetupaccayena
 paccayo:.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. nakusalaṃ. 2 Ma. nakusalo. 3 Ma. (saṅkhittaṃ) evamīdisesu ṭhānesu.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 004]
       [10] Nakusalo dhammo nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena
 paccayo: nakusalo dhammo nākusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena
 paccayo: nakusalo dhammo nābyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena
 paccayo: nakusalo dhammo nakusalassa ca nābyākatassa ca
 dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo: nakusalo dhammo
 nākusalassa ca nābyākatassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo:
 nakusalo dhammo nakusalassa ca nākusalassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena
 paccayo: cha. Nākusalo dhammo nākusalassa dhammassa
 ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo: cha. Nābyākato dhammo
 nābyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo: cha.
 Nakusalo ca nābyākato ca dhammā nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena
 paccayo: cha. Nākusalo ca nābyākato ca dhammā
 nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo: cha. Nakusalo ca
 nākusalo ca dhammā nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena
 paccayo: cha.
      [11] Nakusalo dhammo nakusalassa dhammassa adhipatipaccayena
 paccayo: anantarapaccayena paccayo: samanantara ... Sahajāta ...
 Aññamañña ... Nissaya ... Upanissaya ....
      [12] ... Purejātapaccayena paccayo: nakusalo dhammo
 nākusalassa dhammassa purejātapaccayena paccayo: nakusalo dhammo
-------------------------------------------------
[page 005]
 Nakusalassa ca nākusalassa ca dhammassa purejātapaccayena paccayo: [1]-.
 Nākusalo dhammo nākusalassa dhammassa purejātapaccayena paccayo:
 nākusalo dhammo nakusalassa dhammassa purejātapaccayena paccayo:
 nākusalo dhammo nakusalassa ca nākusalassa ca dhammassa purejātapaccayena
 paccayo: [1]-. Nakusalo ca nākusalo ca dhammā nakusalassa
 dhammassa purejātapaccayena paccayo: nakusalo ca nākusalo ca
 dhammā nākusalassa dhammassa purejātapaccayena paccayo: nakusalo ca
 nākusalo ca dhammā nakusalassa ca nākusalassa ca dhammassa purejātapaccayena
 paccayo: [1]-.
      [13] Hetuyā ekūnattiṃsa ārammaṇe chattiṃsa adhipatiyā
 pañcattiṃsa anantare catuttiṃsa samanantare catuttiṃsa sahajāte
 ekūnattiṃsa aññamaññe catuvīsa nissaye catuttiṃsa upanissaye
 chattiṃsa purejāte aṭṭhārasa pacchājāte aṭṭhārasa āsevane
 catuvīsa kamme ekūnattiṃsa vipāke nava āhāre ekūnattiṃsa
 avigate catuttiṃsa.
       Yathā kusalattike pañhāvārassa anulomampi paccanīyampi
       anulomapaccanīyampi paccanīyānulomampi gaṇitaṃ evaṃ gaṇetabbaṃ.
                      Navedanāttikaṃ 2-
      [14] Nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasukhāyavedanāya-
 sampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. chasaddo dissati. 2 Ma. nasaddo natthi. evamīdisesu ṭhānesu.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 006]
 Dhammaṃ paṭicca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca
 nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nadukkhāya-
 vedanāyasampayutto ca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca
 nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
 Nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: satta. Nādukkhamasukhāya-
 vedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: satta. [1]-
                        Navipākattikaṃ
      [15] Navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā:. Navipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭicca navipākadhammadhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā:. Nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭicca

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. hetuyā ekūnapaññāsa .pe. avigate ekūnapaññāsa. sabbattha īdisameva.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 007]
 Nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:. [1]-
                   Naupādinnupādāniyattikaṃ 2-
      [16] Naupādinnupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca naupādinnupādāniyo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:. Nānupādinnupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nānupādinnupādāniyo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
 Nānupādinnānupādāniyaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānupādinnānupādāniyo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
                  Nasaṅkiliṭṭhasaṅkilesikattikaṃ 3-
      [17] Nasaṅkiliṭṭhasaṅkilesikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaṅkiliṭṭhasaṅkilesiko
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:. Nāsaṅkiliṭṭhasaṅkilesikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nāsaṅkiliṭṭhasaṅkilesiko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
 Nāsaṅkiliṭṭhāsaṅkilesikaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nāsaṅkiliṭṭhāsaṅkilesiko
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
                       Navitakkattikaṃ
      [18] Nasavitakkasavicāraṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasavitakkasavicāro dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā:. Nāvitakkavicāramattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nāvitakkavicāramatto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:. Nāvitakkāvicāraṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nāvitakkāvicāro dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā:.

  Footnote:1 Ma. sabbapabbantesu hetuyā ... avigate ... iti pāṭhā dissanti.
 2 Ma. upādinnattika. 3 Ma. saṅkiliṭṭhattika.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 008]
                         Napītittikaṃ
      [19] Napītisahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca napītisahagato dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nasukhasahagataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Naupekkhāsahagataṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca ....
                        Nadassanattikaṃ
      [20] Nadassanenapahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Nabhāvanāyapahātabbaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Nanevadassanenanabhāvanāyapahātabbaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca ....
                  Nadassanenapahātabbahetukattikaṃ 1-
      [21] Nadassanenapahātabbahetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Nabhāvanāya-
 pahātabbahetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Nanevadassanenanabhāvanāyapahātabbahetukaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca ....
                      Nāacayagāmittikaṃ
      [22] Nāacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Nāpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca ... Nanevācayagāmināpacayagāmiṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ....
                        Nasekkhattikaṃ
      [23] Nasekkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Nāsekkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ...
 Nanevasekkhānāsekkhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ....
                        Naparittattikaṃ
      [24] Naparittaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Namahaggataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ...

                      Naparittārammaṇattikaṃ
      [25] Naparittārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Namahaggatārammaṇaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Nāppamāṇārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ....
                         Nahīnattikaṃ
      [26] Nahīnaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Namajjhimaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ...
 Napaṇītaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ....
                        Namicchattattikaṃ
      [27] Namicchattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Nasammattaniyataṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca ... Nāniyataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ....
                      Namaggārammaṇattikaṃ
      [28] Namaggārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Namaggahetukaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca ... Namaggādhipatiṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ....
                        Nauppannattikaṃ
      [29] Nānuppannaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānuppanno dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nānuppannaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nauppādī dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nānuppannaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānuppanno ca
 nauppādī ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:. Nauppādiṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nauppādī dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nauppādiṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nānuppanno dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
-------------------------------------------------
[page 010]
 Nauppādiṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānuppanno ca nauppādī ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā:. Nānuppannañca nauppādiñca dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nānuppanno dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nānuppannañca
 nauppādiñca dhammaṃ paṭicca nauppādī dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nānuppannañca nauppādiñca dhammaṃ paṭicca nānuppanno ca
 nauppādī ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
                         Nātītattikaṃ
      [30] Nātītaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nātīto dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā:.
      [31] Nātīto dhammo nātītassa dhammassa hetupaccayena
 paccayo: nātīto dhammo nānāgatassa dhammassa hetupaccayena
 paccayo: nātīto dhammo nātītassa ca nānāgatassa ca dhammassa
 hetupaccayena paccayo:. Nānāgato dhammo nānāgatassa dhammassa
 hetupaccayena paccayo: nānāgato dhammo nātītassa dhammassa
 hetupaccayena paccayo: nānāgato dhammo nātītassa ca
 nānāgatassa ca dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo:. Nātīto ca
 nānāgato ca dhammā nātītassa dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo:
 nātīto ca nānāgato ca dhammā nānāgatassa dhammassa
 hetupaccayena paccayo: nātīto ca nānāgato ca dhammā nātītassa
 ca nānāgatassa ca dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo:.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 011]
                      Nātītārammaṇattikaṃ
      [32] Nātītārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Nānāgatārammaṇaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca ... Napaccuppannārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ....
                        Nājjhattattikaṃ
      [33] Nājjhattaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nājjhatto dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā:. Nabahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭicca nabahiddhā dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
                      Nājjhattārammaṇattikaṃ
      [34] Nājjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nājjhattārammaṇo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nājjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nabahiddhārammaṇo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:. Nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nabahiddhārammaṇo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nājjhattārammaṇo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
                       Nasanidassanattikaṃ
      [35] Nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nānidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanāppaṭigho dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho
-------------------------------------------------
[page 012]
 Ca nānidassanāppaṭigho ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanasappaṭigho ca
 nānidassanāppaṭigho ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho ca
 nānidassanasappaṭigho ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
      [36] Nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanasappaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nasanidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanāppaṭigho dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 ca nānidassanāppaṭigho ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanasappaṭigho ca
 nānidassanāppaṭigho ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho ca
 nānidassanāppaṭigho ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
      [37] Nānidassanāppaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanāppaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nānidassanāppaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nasanidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nānidassanāppaṭighaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: cha.

[page 013]
      [38] Nasanidassanasappaṭighañca nānidassanāppaṭighañca dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: cha.
      [39] Nasanidassanasappaṭighañca nānidassanasappaṭighañca dhammaṃ

      [40] Hetuyā tiṃsa avigate tiṃsa.
 Yathā kusalattike sahajātavārampi paccayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi
 sampayuttavārampi pañhāvārampi gaṇitaṃ evaṃ gaṇetabbaṃ.
                  Paccanīya tikapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ.
                    ----------------
                      Paccanīya dukapaṭṭhānaṃ
                         nahetudukaṃ
      [41] Nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ ekaṃ khandhaṃ paṭicca tayo khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañca
 rūpaṃ dve khandhe paṭicca ... Paṭisandhikkhaṇe ... Yāva mahābhūtā.
 Nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nahetū khandhe paṭicca ... Hetupaṭisandhi 1-. Nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nahetu ca nanahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:. Nanahetuṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nanahetuṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nanahetuṃ dhammaṃ

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote:1 Ma. paṭisandhikkhaṇe.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 014]
 Paṭicca nahetu ca nanahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
 Nahetuñca nanahetuñca dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nahetuñca nanahetuñca dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuñca nanahetuñca dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu
 ca nanahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
      [42] Hetuyā nava ārammaṇe nava avigate nava.
            Sahajātavārampi sampayuttavārampi paṭiccavārasadisaṃ
                     evaṃ vitthāretabbaṃ.
      [43] Nanahetu dhammo nanahetussa dhammassa hetupaccayena
 paccayo: nanahetu dhammo nahetussa dhammassa hetupaccayena
 paccayo: nanahetu dhammo nahetussa ca nanahetussa ca dhammassa
 hetupaccayena paccayo:.
      [44] Nahetu dhammo nahetussa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena

      [192] Nahetuṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nakusalo dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ akusalaṃ abyākataṃ ekaṃ khandhaṃ
 paṭicca tayo khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañca rūpaṃ. Nahetuṃ nakusalaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nahetū akusale abyākate khandhe paṭicca hetu. Nahetuṃ nakusalaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nakusalo ca nanahetu nakusalo ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
      [193] Nanahetuṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu nakusalo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nanahetuṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nahetu nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nanahetuṃ nakusalaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nakusalo ca nanahetu nakusalo ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.

                    Nahetudukanasanidassanattikaṃ
      [232] Nahetuṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu
 nasanidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ
 nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nahetu nasanidassanasappaṭigho ca nanahetu nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
      [233] Nanahetuṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu
 nasanidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nanahetuṃ
 nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nanahetuṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nahetu nasanidassanasappaṭigho ca nanahetu nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
      [234] Nahetuṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañca nanahetuṃ
 nasanidassanasappaṭighañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nasanidassanasappaṭigho dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañca nanahetuṃ
 nasanidassanasappaṭighañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu nasanidassanasappaṭigho
-------------------------------------------------
[page 060]
 Dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañca
 nanahetuṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 ca nanahetu nasanidassanasappaṭigho ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā:.
      [235] Hetuyā nava avigate nava sabbattha nava.
      [236] Nahetuṃ nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu
 nānidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ
 nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu nānidassanasappaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nahetu nādidassanasappaṭigho ca nanahetu nānidassanasappaṭigho
 ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
      [237] Nanahetuṃ nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu
 nānidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nanahetuṃ
 nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nānidassanasappaṭigho dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nanahetuṃ nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nahetu nānidassanasappaṭigho ca nanahetu nānidassanasappaṭigho ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
      [238] Nahetuṃ nānidassanasappaṭighañca nanahetuṃ nānidassanasappaṭighañca
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nānidassanasappaṭigho dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ nānidassanasappaṭighañca nanahetuṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 061]
 Nānidassanasappaṭighañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu nānidassanasappaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ nānidassanasappaṭighañca
 nanahetuṃ nānidassanasappaṭighañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nānidassanasappaṭigho
 ca nanahetu nānidassanasappaṭigho ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā:.
      [239] Hetuyā nava avigate nava.
          Sahajātavārepi sampayuttavārepi sabbattha vitthāro.
      [240] Nanahetu nānidassanasappaṭigho dhammo nanahetussa
 nānidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo: nanahetu
 nānidassanasappaṭigho dhammo nahetussa nānidassanasappaṭighassa
 dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo: nanahetu nānidassanasappaṭigho
 dhammo nahetussa nānidassanasappaṭighassa ca nanahetussa nānidassanasappaṭighassa
 ca dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo:.
      [241] Nahetu nānidassanasappaṭigho dhammo nahetussa
 nānidassanasappaṭighassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo:.
      [242] Hetuyā tīṇi ārammaṇe nava avigate nava.
                 Pañhāvāraṃ evaṃ vitthāretabbaṃ.
      [243] Nahetuṃ nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu
 nānidassanāppaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
      [244] Hetuyā ekaṃ sabbattha vitthāro.

[page 062]
                     Nasahetukadukanakusalattikaṃ
      [245] Nasahetukaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasahetuko nakusalo
 dhammo upapajjati hetupaccayā: vicikicchāsahagataṃ uddhaccasahagataṃ
 mohaṃ paṭicca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ ekaṃ mahābhūtaṃ paṭicca tayo
 mahābhūtā. Nasahetukaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nāhetuko nakusalo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: vicikicchāsahagataṃ uddhaccasahagataṃ
 mohaṃ paṭicca sampayuttakā khandhā paṭisandhikkhaṇe vatthuṃ paṭicca
 sahetukā khandhā. Nasahetukaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasahetuko
 nakusalo ca nāhetuko nakusalo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 tīṇi. Nāhetukaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nāhetuko
 nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Nasahetukaṃ
 nakusalañca nāhetukaṃ nakusalañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nasahetuko nakusalo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [246] Hetuyā nava.
      [247] Nasahetukaṃ nākusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasahetuko nākusalo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Nāhetukaṃ nākusalaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nāhetuko nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 tīṇi. Nasahetukaṃ nākusalañca nāhetukaṃ nākusalañca dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nasahetuko nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [248] Hetuyā nava.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 063]
      [249] Nasahetukaṃ nābyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nāhetuko
 nābyākato dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: ekaṃ. Nāhetukaṃ
 nābyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nāhetuko nābyākato dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: ekaṃ. Nasahetukaṃ nābyākatañca nāhetukaṃ nābyākatañca

 hetupaccayā: ekaṃ.
      [250] Hetuyā tīṇi.
              Nahetusampayuttadukaṃ nahetu nasahetukadukasadisaṃ.
                   Nahetusahetukadukanakusalattikaṃ
      [251] Nahetuñcevanāhetukañca nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nahetucevanāhetukoca nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nahetuñcevanāhetukañca nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nāhetukocevananacahetu
 nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuñcevanāhetukañca
 nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetucevanāhetukoca
 nakusalo ca nāhetukocevananacahetu nakusalo ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Nāhetukañcevananacahetuṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nāhetukocevananacahetu nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 tīṇi. Nahetuñcevanāhetukañca nakusalañca
 nāhetukañcevananacahetuṃ nakusalañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetuceva
 nāhetuko nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi.

[page 064]
      [252] Hetuyā nava.
      [253] Nahetuñcevanāhetukañca nākusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca

      [254] Hetuyā nava.
      [255] Nahetuñcevanāhetukañca nābyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nahetucevanāhetukoca nābyākato dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
      [256] Hetuyā nava.
 Nahetuceva nahetuvippayuttadukaṃ nahetuceva nahetusampayuttadukasadisaṃ.
                   Nahetunasahetukadukanakusalattikaṃ
      [257] Nahetuṃ nasahetukaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nasahetuko
 nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nava. Nahetuṃ nasahetukaṃ
 nākusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nasahetuko nākusalo ... Nava.
 Nahetuṃ nāhetukaṃ nābyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nāhetuko

 upāyena kusalākusaladukaṃ kusalattikameva ettha vaṭṭati.

      [265] Nasaraṇaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaraṇo nakusalo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:. Nāraṇaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nāraṇo nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nāraṇaṃ
 nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaraṇo nakusalo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nāraṇaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaraṇo nakusalo ca
 nāraṇo nakusalo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:. Nasaraṇaṃ
 nakusalañca nāraṇaṃ nakusalañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaraṇo nakusalo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
      [266] Hetuyā pañca avigate pañca.
      [267] Nasaraṇaṃ nākusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaraṇo nākusalo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.

[page 069]
      [268] Hetuyā ekaṃ sabbattha ekaṃ.

 nāraṇo nābyākato dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.

                    Nasaraṇaduka nasanidassanattikaṃ
      [274] Nasaraṇaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaraṇo
-------------------------------------------------
[page 070]
 Nasanidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nasaraṇaṃ
 nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nāraṇo nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nasaraṇaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nasaraṇo nasanidassanasappaṭigho ca nāraṇo nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:. Nāraṇaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nāraṇo nasanidassanasappaṭigho dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nāraṇaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nasaraṇo nasanidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nāraṇaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaraṇo nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 ca nāraṇo nasanidassanasappaṭigho ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā:. Nasaraṇaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañca nāraṇaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañca
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaraṇo nasanidassanasappaṭigho dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [275] Hetuyā nava.
      [276] Nasaraṇaṃ nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaraṇo
 nānidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:. Nāraṇaṃ
 nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nāraṇo nānidassanasappaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nāraṇaṃ nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nasaraṇo nānidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nāraṇaṃ nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaraṇo
-------------------------------------------------
[page 071]
 Nānidassanasappaṭigho ca nāraṇo nānidassanasappaṭigho ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā:. Nasaraṇaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighañca nāraṇaṃ
 nānidassanasappaṭighañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaraṇo nānidassanasappaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
      [277] Hetuyā pañca ārammaṇe dve avigate pañca.
         Sahajātavārepi pañhāvārepi sabbattha vitthāretabbaṃ.

                  Paccanīya dukattikapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ.

                     Paccanīyatikadukapaṭṭhānaṃ
                      nakusalattikanahetudukaṃ
      [280] Nakusalaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nahetu dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nābyākato nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ
 nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nahetu ca nābyākato nahetu ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
-------------------------------------------------
[page 072]
 Nakusalo nahetu ca nākusalo nahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: pañca.
      [281] Nākusalaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nahetu dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nākusalaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nākusalaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nābyākato nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nākusalaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nahetu ca nābyākato
 nahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nākusalaṃ nahetuṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nahetu ca nākusalo nahetu ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca.
      [282] Nābyākataṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato nahetu
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nābyākataṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nakusalo nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nābyākataṃ
 nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nābyākataṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nahetu ca
 nābyākato nahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 nābyākataṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nahetu ca nābyākato
 nahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nābyākataṃ nahetuṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nahetu ca nākusalo nahetu ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: cha.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 073]
      [283] Nakusalaṃ nahetuñca nābyākataṃ nahetuñca dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nakusalo nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nahetuñca
 nābyākataṃ nahetuñca dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nahetu dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nahetuñca nābyākataṃ nahetuñca
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 pañca.
      [284] Nākusalaṃ nahetuñca nābyākataṃ nahetuñca dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nakusalo nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nākusalaṃ
 nahetuñca nābyākataṃ nahetuñca dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nahetu
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nākusalaṃ nahetuñca nābyākataṃ
 nahetuñcadhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato nahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: pañca.
      [285] Nakusalaṃ nahetuñca nākusalaṃ nahetuñca dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nakusalo nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nahetuñca
 nākusalaṃ nahetuñca dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nahetu dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nahetuñca nākusalaṃ nahetuñca dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nakusalo nahetu ca nākusalo nahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [286] Hetu yā ekūnattiṃsa ārammaṇe catuvīsa avigate
 ekūnattiṃsa sabbattha vitthāro.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 074]
      [287] Nakusalaṃ nanahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nanahetu dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nanahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo
 nanahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nanahetuṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nābyākato nanahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nakusalaṃ nanahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nanahetu ca nābyākato
 nanahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nanahetuṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nanahetu ca nākusalo na nahetu ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca. Nākusalaṃ nanahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nākusalo nanahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: pañca.
 Nābyākataṃ nanahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato nanahetu dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: pañca. Nakusalaṃ nanahetuñca nābyākataṃ
 nanahetuñca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nanahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Nākusalaṃ nanahetuñca nābyākataṃ nanahetuñca
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nanahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 tīṇi. Nakusalaṃ nanahetuñca nākusalaṃ nanahetuñca dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nakusalo nanahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [288] Hetuyā catuvīsa ārammaṇe catuvīsa avigate catuvīsa
 sabbattha vitthāro.
                    Nakusalattika nasahetukadukaṃ
      [289] Nakusalaṃ nasahetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nasahetuko

[page 075]

 nākusalo nasahetuko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ

 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ
 nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:. Nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ
 nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:. Nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ
 nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca ....

                    Nasanidassanattikanahetudukaṃ
      [327] Nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ
 nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanasappaṭigho nahetu dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nānidassanāppaṭigho nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā : cha.
 Nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanasappaṭigho
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahetuṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho nahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanāppaṭigho
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: cha. Nānidassanāppaṭighaṃ
 nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanāppaṭigho nahetu
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: cha. Nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ
 nahetuñca nānidassanāppaṭighaṃ nahetuñca dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanasappaṭigho
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: cha. Nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ
 nahetuñca anidassanasappaṭighaṃ nahetuñca dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nasanidassanasappaṭigho nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: cha.
      [328] Hetuyā tiṃsa ārammaṇe nava sabbattha vitthāro.

                     Paccanīyatikattikapaṭṭhānaṃ
                    nakusalattikanavedanāttikaṃ
      [346] Nakusalaṃ nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nakusalo nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
-------------------------------------------------
[page 091]
 Nakusalaṃ nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo
 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ
 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato nasukhāyavedanāya-
 sampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nasukhāyavedanāya-
 sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 ca nābyākato nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nakusalo nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca nakusalo
 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca.
      [347] Nākusalaṃ nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nākusalo nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nākusalaṃ nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo
 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nākusalaṃ

 nābyākato nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca dhammā uppajjanti

      [352] Hetuyā ekūnattiṃsa ārammaṇe catuvīsa avigate
 ekūnattiṃsa.
      Sahajātavārepi pañhāvārepi vitthāro.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 094]
      [353] Nakusalaṃ nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nakusalo nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nakusalaṃ nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo
 nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ
 nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato
 nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ
 nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nadukkhāyavedanāya-
 sampayutto ca nābyākato nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca nākusalo
 nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 pañca. Nākusalaṃ nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nākusalo nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: pañca. Nābyākataṃ nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nābyākato nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: cha. Nakusalaṃ nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttañca
 nābyākataṃ nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo
 nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: pañca.
 Nākusalaṃ nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttañca nābyākataṃ nadukkhāyavedanāya-
 sampayuttañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto
-------------------------------------------------
[page 095]
 Sampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: pañca. Nakusalaṃ
 nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttañca nākusalaṃ nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttañca
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [354] Hetuyā ekūnattiṃsa avigate ekūnattiṃsa sabbattha
 vitthāretabbaṃ.
      [355] Nakusalaṃ nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nakusalo nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: pañca. Nākusalaṃ nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: pañca. Nābyākataṃ nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāya-
 sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāya-
 sampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: cha. Nakusalaṃ
 nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttañca nābyākataṃ nādukkhamasukhāya-
 vedanāyasampayuttañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nādukkhamasukhāya-
 vedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: pañca.
 Nākusalaṃ nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttañca nābyākataṃ
 nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo
 nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 pañca. Nakusalaṃ nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttañca nākusalaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 096]
 Nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo
 nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [356] Hetuyā ekūnattiṃsa avigate ekūnattiṃsa sabbattha
 vitthāro.
                     Nakusalattikanavipākattikaṃ
      [357] Nakusalaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo navipāko dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo
 navipāko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nābyākato navipāko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nakusalaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo navipāko ca nābyākato
 navipāko ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ navipākaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo navipāko ca nākusalo navipāko ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca. Nākusalaṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nākusalo navipāko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: pañca.
 Nābyākataṃ navipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato navipāko dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: cha. Nakusalaṃ navipākañca nābyākataṃ
 navipākañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo navipāko dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: pañca. Nākusalaṃ navipākañca nābyākataṃ
 navipākañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo navipāko dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: pañca. Nakusalaṃ navipākañca nākusalaṃ navipākañca dhammaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 097]
 Paṭicca nakusalo navipāko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [358] Hetuyā ekūnattiṃsa avigate ekūnattiṃsa sabbattha
 vitthāro.
      [359] Nakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo
 navipākadhammadhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammaṃ
 paṭicca nākusalo navipākadhammadhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nakusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo navipākadhammadhammo ca
 nākusalo navipākadhammadhammo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.

 navipākadhammadhammo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:. Nakusalaṃ
 navipākadhammadhammañca nākusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammañca dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nakusalo navipākadhammadhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ
 navipākadhammadhammañca nākusalaṃ navipākadhammadhammañca dhammaṃ paṭicca

 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.

      [380] Nakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo
 nasanidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ
 nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nābyākato nasanidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo
 nasanidassanasappaṭigho ca nābyākato nasanidassanasappaṭigho ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nakusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nakusalo nasanidassanasappaṭigho ca nākusalo nasanidassanasappaṭigho ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca.

 nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nasanidassanasappaṭigho
-------------------------------------------------
[page 102]
 Dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nākusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato nasanidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nākusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo

 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: nākusalaṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nakusalo nasanidassanasappaṭigho ca nākusalo nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca.
      [382] Nābyākataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca

 nābyākataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nābyākataṃ nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ

 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: cha.

 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: satta.
      [392] Nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca

 nakusalañca nādukakhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ nakusalañca
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto nakusalo dhammo

 nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:

      [395] Nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ nakusalañca nādukkhamasukhāya-
 vedanāyasampayuttaṃ nakusalañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto

 hetupaccayā:.
      [396] Hetuyā ekūnapaññāsa ārammaṇe ekūnapaññāsa
 avigate ekūnapaññāsa.
      [397] Nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ nākusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
      [398] Hetuyā ekūnapaññāsa.
      [399] Nasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ nābyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto nābyākato dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā:.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 107]
      [400] Hetuyā aṭṭhacattālīsa sabbattha vitthāro.

 hetupaccayā:.
      [402] Hetuyā ekūnapaññāsa avigate ekūnapaññāsa.

                    Nasanidassanattikanakusalattikaṃ
      [422] Nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ
 nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanasappaṭigho nakusalo
-------------------------------------------------
[page 111]
 Dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nānidassanāppaṭigho nakusalo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: cha. Nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nānidassanasappaṭigho nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanāppaṭigho nakusalo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: cha. Nānidassanāppaṭighaṃ nakusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nānidassanāppaṭigho nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 cha. Nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalañca nānidassanāppaṭighaṃ
 nakusalañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho nakusalo dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: cha. Nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ nakusalañca
 nānidassanāppaṭighaṃ nakusalañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: cha.
      [423] Hetuyā tiṃsa ārammaṇe nava sabbattha vitthāro.
      [424] Nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nākusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
      [425] Hetuyā tiṃsa ārammaṇe nava sabbattha vitthāro.
      [426] Nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nābyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho

 Nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhā dhammaṃ paṭicca ....
-------------------------------------------------
[page 116]
                Nasanidassanattikanājjhattārammaṇattikaṃ
      [447] Nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nājjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nasanidassanasappaṭigho nājjhattārammaṇo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nājjhattārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nānidassanasappaṭigho nājjhattārammaṇo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā:.
      [448] Hetuyā tiṃsa avigate tiṃsa sabbattha vitthāro.
      [449] Nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nasanidassanasappaṭigho nabahiddhārammaṇo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nānidassanasappaṭigho nabahiddhārammaṇo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nānidassanāppaṭigho nabahiddhārammaṇo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: cha.
      [450] Nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nasanidassanasappaṭigho nabahiddhārammaṇo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: cha.
      [451] Nānidassanāppaṭighaṃ nabahiddhārammaṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nānidassanāppaṭigho nabahiddhārammaṇo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: cha.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 117]
      [452] Nasanidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhārammaṇañca nānidassanāppaṭighaṃ
 nabahiddhārammaṇañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 nabahiddhārammaṇo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: cha.
      [453] Nānidassanasappaṭighaṃ nabahiddhārammaṇañca nānidassanāppaṭighaṃ

        sampayuttavārepi pañhāvārepi vitthāro.
                  Paccanīyatikattikapaṭṭhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ.
                       ----------
                     Paccanīyadukadukapaṭṭhānaṃ
                     nahetudukanasahetukadukaṃ
      [455] Nahetuṃ nasahetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nasahetuko
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
      [456] Hetuyā tīṇi ārammaṇe ekaṃ avigate tīṇi.
      [457] Nahetuṃ nasahetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nasahetuko
 dhammo uppajjati nahetupaccayā:.
      [458] Nahetuṃ nasahetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nasahetuko
-------------------------------------------------
[page 118]
 Dhammo uppajjati nāarammaṇapaccayā:.
      [459] Nahetuyā ekaṃ nāarammaṇe tīṇi nādhipatiyā nava.
      [460] Nahetu nasahetuko dhammo nahetussa nasahetukassa
 dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo:.
      [461] Nahetu nasahetuko dhammo nahetussa nasahetukassa
 dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo: nahetu nasahetuko dhammo
 nanahetussa nasahetukassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo:.
      [462] Hetuyā ekaṃ ārammaṇe cattāri avigate cattāri.
      [463] Nahetuṃ nāhetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nāhetuko
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ nāhetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nanahetu nāhetuko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ
 nāhetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nāhetuko ca nanahetu nāhetuko ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [464] Nanahetuṃ nāhetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu nāhetuko
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nanahetuṃ nāhetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nahetu nāhetuko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nanahetuṃ
 nāhetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nāhetuko ca nanahetu nāhetuko ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [465] Nahetuṃ nāhetukañca nanahetuṃ nāhetukañca dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nahetu nāhetuko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
-------------------------------------------------
[page 119]
 Nahetuṃ nāhetukañca nanahetuṃ nāhetukañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu
 nāhetuko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ nāhetukañca
 nanahetuṃ nāhetukañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nāhetuko ca nanahetu
 nāhetuko ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [466] Hetuyā nava ārammaṇe nava avigate nava sabbattha
 nava.
                    Nahetudukanahetusampayuttadukaṃ
      [467] Nahetuṃ nahetusampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu
 nahetusampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Nahetuṃ
 nahetuvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nahetuvippayutto dhammo
 upapajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Nanahetuṃ nahetuvippayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nanahetu nahetuvippayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [468] Hetuyā nava sabbattha vitthāro.
                    Nahetudukanahetusahetukadukaṃ
      [469] Nahetuṃ nahetuñcevanāhetukañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu
 nahetucevanāhetukoca dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:. Yāva
 pañhāvārepi ekaṃ. Nanahetuṃ nāhetukañcevananacahetuṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nanahetu nāhetukocevananacahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā:.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 120]
                  Nahetudukanahetuhetusampayuttadukaṃ
      [470] Nahetuṃ nahetuñcevanahetuvippayuttañca dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nahetu nahetucevanahetuvippayuttoca dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
 Nanahetuṃ nahetuvippayuttañcevananacahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu
 nahetuvippayuttocevananacahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
                   Nahetudukanahetunasahetukadukaṃ
      [471] Nahetuṃ nahetuṃ nasahetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nahetunasahetuko
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:. Nahetuṃ nahetuṃ
 nāhetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nahetu nāhetuko dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā:.
                      Nahetudukacūḷantaradukaṃ
      [472] Nahetuṃ nāppaccayaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nāppaccayo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ nāppaccayaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nanahetu nāppaccayo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ
 nāppaccayaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nāppaccayo ca nanahetu
 nāppaccayo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
      [473] Nanahetuṃ nāppaccayaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu
 nāppaccayo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nanahetuṃ nāppaccayaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nāppaccayo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:

      [518] Hetuyā ekaṃ sabbattha vitthāro.
      [519] Nasaraṇaṃ nānuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaraṇo nānuttaro
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: ekaṃ. Nāraṇaṃ nānuttaraṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nāraṇo nānuttaro dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nāraṇaṃ nānuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaraṇo nānuttaro dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nāraṇaṃ nānuttaraṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasaraṇo
 nānuttaro ca nāraṇo nānuttaro ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā:. Nasaraṇaṃ nānuttarañca nāraṇaṃ nānuttarañca dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nasaraṇo nānuttaro dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
      [520] Hetuyā pañca sabbattha vitthāro.
        Sahajātavārampi paccayavārampi nissayavārampi saṃsaṭṭhavārampi
            sampayuttavārampi pañhāvārampi vitthāretabbaṃ.

                     Abhidhammapiṭake paṭṭhānaṃ
                     anulomapaccanīyapaṭṭhānaṃ

             namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa
                    anulomapaccanīyatikapaṭṭhānaṃ
                     kusalattikenakusalattikaṃ
      [1] Kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: kusale khandhe paṭicca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ. Kusalaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: kusalaṃ
 ekaṃ khandhaṃ paṭicca tayo khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañca rūpaṃ. Kusalaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: kusalaṃ
 ekaṃ khandhaṃ paṭicca tayo khandhā. Kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo ca
 nābyākato ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: kusalaṃ ekaṃ
 khandhaṃ paṭicca tayo khandhā. Kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo ca
 nākusalo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: kusale khandhe
 paṭicca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ. Pañca.
      [2] Akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: akusale khandhe paṭicca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ. Akusalaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: akusalaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 136]
 Ekaṃ khandhaṃ paṭicca tayo khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañca rūpaṃ. Akusalaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: akusalaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo ca nābyākato ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo ca nākusalo ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:. Pañca.
      [3] Abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ ekaṃ khandhaṃ paṭicca
 tayo khandhā cittasamuṭṭhānañca rūpaṃ paṭisandhi ekaṃ mahābhūtaṃ
 paṭicca tayo mahābhūtā dve mahābhūte paṭicca dve mahābhūtā
 mahābhūte paṭicca cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ. Abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: vipākābyākataṃ paṭisandhi
 mahābhūtaṃ. Abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo ca nākusalo ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: vipākābyākataṃ kiriyābyākataṃ.
 Saṅkhittaṃ. Tīṇi.
      [4] Kusalañca abyākatañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: kusale khandhe ca mahābhūte ca paṭicca
 cittasamuṭṭhānaṃ rūpaṃ. Kusalañca abyākatañca dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: kusalañca abyākatañca
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo ca nākusalo ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā:.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 137]

 nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: akusalañca abyākatañca
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo ca nākusalo ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: cittasamuṭṭhānarūpameva ettha vattati.
                  Ekūnavīsati pañhā kātabbā.

 Ekūnavīsa.
      [10] Akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo dhammo uppajjati

      [14] Kusalo dhammo nakusalassa dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo:
 kusalo dhammo nākusalassa dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo: kusalo
 dhammo nābyākatassa dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo: kusalo
 dhammo nakusalassa ca nābyākatassa ca dhammassa hetupaccayena
 paccayo: kusalo dhammo nakusalassa ca nākusalassa ca dhammassa
 hetupaccayena paccayo: pañca.

      [18] Hetuyā terasa ārammaṇe aṭṭhārasa adhipatiyā
 sattarasa anantare soḷasa samanantare soḷasa sahajāte ekūnavīsa
 aññamaññe nava nissaye chabbīsa upanissaye aṭṭhārasa
 purejāte cha pacchājāte nava āsevane nava kamme terasa
 vipāke tīṇi āhāre terasa .pe. Magge terasa sampayutte
 nava vippayutte dvādasa .pe. Avigate chabbīsa.
                   Pañhāvāraṃ vitthāretabbaṃ.
                   Vedanāttikenavedanāttikaṃ
      [19] Sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: sukhāyavedanāyasampayutte
 khandhe paṭicca sukhavedanā cittasamuṭṭhānañca rūpaṃ paṭisandhikkhaṇe
 mahābhūtā natthi. Sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:

 rūpaṃ. Sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto

 dhammaṃ paṭicca nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto
-------------------------------------------------
[page 141]
 Ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 ca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 ca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā:. Satta.
      [20] Dukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca

      [27] Vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipākadhammadhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipākadhammadhammo
 ca nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 vipākaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko ca navipākadhammadhammo ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca.
      [28] Vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭicca navipākadhammadhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭicca nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko
 ca nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 vipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko ca navipākadhammadhammo ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca.
      [29] Nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭicca nanevavipākanavipāka-
 dhammadhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 145]
 Paṭicca navipāko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ
 paṭicca navipākadhammadhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭicca navipākadhammadhammo ca
 nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko ca navipākadhammadhammo ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca.
      [30] Vipākañca nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammañca dhammaṃ paṭicca
 navipāko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: vipākañca

      [214] Saraṇo dhammo nasaraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena
 paccayo: saraṇo dhammo nāraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena
 paccayo: .pe.
      [215] Hetuyā cattāri ārammaṇe cattāri adhipatiyā
 pañca anantare cattāri avigate pañca.
      [216] Saraṇo dhammo nasaraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena
 paccayo: ... Sahajātapaccayena paccayo: ... Upanissayapaccayena paccayo:
 ... Pacchājātapaccayena paccayo: ... Kammapaccayena paccayo:.
      [217] Saraṇo dhammo nāraṇassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena
 paccayo: ... Sahajātapaccayena paccayo: ... Upanissayapaccayena paccayo:
                          .pe.
      [218] Nahetuyā satta nāarammaṇe satta nādhipatiyā
 satta nānantare satta novigate cattāri.
      [219] Hetupaccayā nāarammaṇe cattāri.
      [220] Nahetupaccayā ārammaṇe cattāri.
           Yathā kusalattike pañhāvāraṃ evaṃ vitthāretabbaṃ.

       [261] Sahetukaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasahetuko nakusalo dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: ekaṃ. Sahetukaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca

 uppajjati hetupaccayā: ekaṃ.
                   Hetusampayuttadukakusalattike
                   nahetusampayuttadukanakusalattikaṃ
      [262] Hetusampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetusampayutto
-------------------------------------------------
[page 198]
 Nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: ekaṃ. Hetusampayuttaṃ
 akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetusampayutto nākusalo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Hetuvippayuttaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paccayā
 nahetuvippayutto nābyākato dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: ekaṃ.
                    Hetusahetukadukakusalattike
                    nahetusahetukadukanakusalattikaṃ
      [263] Hetucevasahetukoca kusalo dhammo nahetussacevanāhetukassaca
 nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo:
 ekaṃ. Hetucevasahetukoca kusalo dhammo nāhetukassacevananacahetussa
 nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo: dve.
 Hetucevasahetukoca kusalo dhammo nahetussacevanāhetukassaca
 nakusalassa ca nāhetukassacevananacahetussa nakusalassa ca dhammassa
 ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo: tīṇi.
      [264] Sahetukocevanacahetu kusalo dhammo
 nāhetukassacevananacahetussa nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo:
 cattāri. ... Nahetussacevanāhetukassaca nakusalassa dhammassa
 ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo: pañca. ... Nahetussacevanāhetukassaca
 nakusalassa ca nāhetukassacevananacahetussa
 nakusalassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo: cha.
      [265] Hetucevasahetukoca kusalo ca sahetukocevanacahetu
-------------------------------------------------
[page 199]
 Kusalo ca dhammā nahetussacevanāhetukassaca nakusalassa dhammassa
 ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo: satta. ... Nāhetukassacevananacahetussa
 nakusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo: aṭṭha.
 ... Hetussacevanāhetukassaca nakusalassa ca nāhetukassacevananacahetussa
 nakusalassa ca dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo: nava.
      [266] Hetucevasahetukoca akusalo dhammo nahetussacevanāhetukassaca
 nākusalassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena paccayo:
 etena upāyena navapañhā kātabbā.
      [267] Hetucevasahetukoca abyākato dhammo
 nahetussacevanāhetukassaca nābyākatassa dhammassa ārammaṇapaccayena
 paccayo: navapañhā kātabbā. Saṅkhittaṃ.
           Hetucevahetusampayuttadukaṃ hetucevasahetukadukasadisaṃ.
                    Saṅkhittaṃ. Navapañhā.
                   Nahetusahetukadukakusalattike
                   nahetunasahetukadukanakusalattikaṃ
      [268] Nahetuṃ sahetukaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nasahetuko
 nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: ekaṃ. Nahetuṃ sahetukaṃ
 akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nasahetuko nākusalo dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: ekaṃ. Nahetuṃ ahetukaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ
 paccayā nahetu nāhetuko nābyākato dhammo uppajjati
-------------------------------------------------
[page 200]
 Hetupaccayā: ekaṃ.
            Sappaccayadukakusalattike nasappaccayadukanakusalattikaṃ
      [269] Sappaccayaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nāppaccayo nakusalo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: ekaṃ. Sappaccayaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nāppaccayo nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 ekaṃ. Sappaccayaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paccayā nāppaccayo
 nābyākato dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: ekaṃ.
                    Saṅkhataṃ sappaccayasadisaṃ.
             Sanidassanadukakusalattike nasanidassanadukanakusalattikaṃ
      [270] Anidassanaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassano nakusalo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Akusalaṃ kusalasadisaṃ.
 Anidassanaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paccayā nasanidassano nābyākato
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
                     Sappaṭighadukakusalattike
                     nasappaṭighadukanakusalattikaṃ
      [271] Appaṭighaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nāppaṭigho nakusalo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Appaṭighaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nāppaṭigho nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 tīṇi. Abyākate ekaṃ.

                Rūpīdukakusalattike narūpīdukanakusalattikaṃ
      [272] Arūpiṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nārūpī nakusalo dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: ekaṃ. Arūpī akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nārūpī nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: ekaṃ. Rūpiṃ
 abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paccayā nārūpī nābyākato dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: ekaṃ.
              Lokiyadukakusalattike nalokiyadukanakusalattikaṃ
      [273] Lokiyaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nalokuttaro nakusalo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: dve. Lokiyaṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nalokuttaro nākusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: ekaṃ.
 Lokiyaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paccayā nalokiyo nābyākato dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: dve.

 ekaṃ. Sāsavaṃ abyākataṃ dhammaṃ paccayā nasāsavo nābyākato
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: dve.

 aniyataṃ akusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca naniyato nākusalo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: dve. Abyākatamūlaṃ dve.
                     Sauttaradukakusalattike
                     nasauttaradukanakusalattikaṃ
      [313] Sauttaraṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānuttaro nakusalo dhammo

 nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: dve. Sauttaraṃ akusalaṃ

 Ekaṃ. Abyākatamūlaṃ dve.

      [348] Kusalaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: kusalaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nahetu dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: kusalaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: kusalaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nākusalo nahetu ca nābyākato nahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: kusalaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nahetu ca
 nākusalo nahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca.
      [349] Akusalaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nahetu dhammo
-------------------------------------------------
[page 226]
 Uppajjati hetupaccayā: akusalaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: akusalaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nābyākato nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 akusalaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nahetu ca nābyākato
 nahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: akusalaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nakusalo nahetu ca nākusalo nahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: pañca.

 hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [351] Hetuyā terasa ārammaṇe nava avigate terasa.

      [361] Kusalo sahetuko dhammo nakusalassa nasahetukassa dhammassa
 hetupaccayena paccayo: kusalo sahetuko dhammo nākusalassa
 nasahetukassa dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo: kusalo sahetuko dhammo
 nakusalassa nasahetukassa ca nākusalassa nasahetukassa ca dhammassa
 hetupaccayena paccayo: tīṇi. Akusalo sahetuko dhammo
 nākusalassa nasahetukassa dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo: akusalo

-------------------------------------------------
[page 229]
 Nākusalassa nasahetukassa ca dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo: tīṇi.
 Abyākato sahetuko dhammo nakusalassa nasahetukassa dhammassa
 hetupaccayena paccayo: abyākato sahetuko dhammo nākusalassa
 nasahetukassa dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo: abyākato sahetuko
 dhammo nakusalassa nasahetukassa ca nākusalassa nasahetukassa ca
 dhammassa hetupaccayena paccayo: tīṇi.
      [362] Hetuyā nava ārammaṇe paṇṇarasa .pe.
                     Avigate ekādasa.
      [363] Akusalaṃ ahetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nāhetuko dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: akusalaṃ ahetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato
 nāhetuko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: akusalaṃ ahetukaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nakusalo nāhetuko ca nābyākato nāhetuko ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Abyākataṃ ahetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nakusalo nāhetuko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: abyākataṃ
 ahetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nāhetuko dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: abyākataṃ ahetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nāhetuko ca
 nākusalo nāhetuko ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: tīṇi.

 cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nābyākato nacittasaṃsaṭṭho dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: kusalaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo
 nacittasaṃsaṭṭho ca nābyākato nacittasaṃsaṭṭho ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: kusalaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nacittasaṃsaṭṭho
 ca nākusalo nacittasaṃsaṭṭho ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: pañca. Akusalaṃ cittasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo

             Vedanāttikahetuduke navedanāttikanahetudukaṃ
      [460] Sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasukhāyavedanāya-
 sampayutto nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: sukhāyavedanāya-
 sampayuttaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ
 hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto nahetu dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: satta. Dukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ hetuṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto nahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: satta. Adukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto nahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: satta.
      [461] Hetuyā ekavīsa avigate ekavīsa sabbattha ekavīsa.
      [462] Sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nadukkhāyavedanāya-
 sampayutto nanahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ
 nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 nanahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ
 nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 nanahetu ca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto nanahetu
 ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Dukkhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ
 nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto nanahetu
-------------------------------------------------
[page 256]
 Dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Adukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ
 nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto nanahetu
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [463] Hetuyā nava.
              Vipākattikahetuduke navipākattikanahetudukaṃ
      [464] Vipākaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko nahetu dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: vipākaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipākadhammadhammo
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: vipākaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo nahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: vipākaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipākadhammadhammo
 nahetu ca nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo nahetu ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: vipākaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko nahetu ca
 navipākadhammadhammo nahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 pañca. Vipākadhammadhammaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipākadhammadhammo
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: vipākadhammadhammaṃ hetuṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 vipākadhammadhammaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: vipākadhammadhammaṃ hetuṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko nahetu ca nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo
 nahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: vipākadhammadhammaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 257]
 Hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko nahetu ca navipākadhammadhammo nahetu
 ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca. Nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ
 hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko nahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 navipākadhammadhammo nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko nahetu ca
 navipākadhammadhammo nahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 tīṇi.
       [465] Hetuyā terasa.
       [466] Vipākaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipākadhammadhammo nanahetu
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: vipākaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo nanahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 vipākaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipākadhammadhammo nanahetu
 ca nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo nanahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Vipākadhammadhammaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 navipāko nanahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: vipākadhammadhammaṃ
 nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo nanahetu dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: vipākadhammadhammaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 navipāko nanahetu ca nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo nanahetu ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 258]
 Nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanevavipākanavipākadhammadhammo nanahetu
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ nahetuṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko nanahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 .pe. Nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca navipāko
 nanahetu ca navipākadhammadhammo nanahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: pañca. Vipākaṃ nahetuñca nevavipākanavipākadhammadhammaṃ
 nahetuñca dhammaṃ paṭicca navipākadhammadhammo nanahetu dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [467] Hetuyā cuddasa.
                  Upādinnupādāniyattikahetuduke
                  naupādinnupādāniyattikanahetudukaṃ
      [468] Upādinnupādāniyaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca naupādinnupādāniyo
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: upādinnupādāniyaṃ
 hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānupādinnupādāniyo nahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: upādinnupādāniyaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānupādinnānupādāniyo
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: upādinnupādāniyaṃ
 hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca naupādinnupādāniyo nahetu ca
 nānupādinnānupādāniyo nahetu ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 upādinnupādāniyaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānupādinnupādāniyo
 nahetu ca nānupādinnānupādāniyo nahetu ca dhammā
-------------------------------------------------
[page 259]
 Uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca. Anupādinnupādāniyaṃ hetuṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca naupādinnupādāniyo nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 tīṇi. Anupādinnānupādāniyaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānupādinnānupādāniyo
 nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: pañca.

 sāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paccayā nānidassanasappaṭigho nasāsavo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: anidassanāppaṭighaṃ sāsavaṃ dhammaṃ
 paccayā nasanidassanasappaṭigho nasāsavo ca nānidassanasappaṭigho
 nasāsavo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
      [581] Hetuyā tīṇi.
      [582] Anidassanāppaṭighaṃ anāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho
 nānāsavo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: anidassanāppaṭighaṃ
 anāsavaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nānidassanasappaṭigho nānāsavo
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: .pe. Anidassanasappaṭighaṃ anāsavaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nasanidassanasappaṭigho nānāsavo ca nānidassanasappaṭigho
 nānāsavo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
      [583] Hetuyā cha.

 nāvicāro ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.

      [714] Kusalaṃ sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo
 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: kusalaṃ
 sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: .pe. Kusalaṃ sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 295]
 Dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 ca nākusalo nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca.
      [715] Akusalaṃ sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo
 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: akusalaṃ
 sukhāyavedanāya sampayuttaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: .pe. Akusalaṃ sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 ca nākusalo nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: pañca.

 nakusalo nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:

           Kusalattikasanidassanattike nakusalattikanasanidassanattikaṃ
      [830] Abyākato sanidassanasappaṭigho dhammo nābyākatassa

      [831] Ārammaṇe cha.
                    Tīṇi veditakaṃ kātabbaṃ.
      [832] Abyākataṃ anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo
 nānidassanasappaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: abyākataṃ
 anidassanasappaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nānidassanasappaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: abyākataṃ anidassanasappaṭighaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nānidassanasappaṭigho ca nākusalo
 nānidassanasappaṭigho ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
      [833] Hetuyā tīṇi.
      [834] Kusalaṃ anidassanāppaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo
 nānidassanāppaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: kusalaṃ anidassanāppaṭighaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nānidassanāppaṭigho dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: kusalaṃ anidassanāppaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
-------------------------------------------------
[page 310]
 Nakusalo nānidassanāppaṭigho ca nākusalo nānidassanāppaṭigho ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Akusalaṃ anidassanāppaṭighaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nānidassanāppaṭigho dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: akusalaṃ anidassanāppaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nakusalo nānidassanāppaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 akusalaṃ anidassanāppaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nānidassanāppaṭigho
 ca nākusalo nānidassanāppaṭigho ca dhammā uppajjanti
 hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Abyākataṃ anidassanāppaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nakusalo nānidassanāppaṭigho dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 abyākataṃ anidassanāppaṭighaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nākusalo nānidassanāppaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: abyākataṃ anidassanāppaṭighaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nānidassanāppaṭigho ca nākusalo
 nānidassanāppaṭigho ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
 Kusalaṃ anidassanāppaṭighañca abyākataṃ anidassanāppaṭighañca dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nakusalo nānidassanāppaṭigho dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Akusalaṃ anidassanāppaṭighañca abyākataṃ
 anidassanāppaṭighañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nakusalo nānidassanāppaṭigho
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [835] Hetuyā paṇṇarasa.

[page 311]
            Vedanāttikakusalattike navedanāttikanakusalattikaṃ
      [836] Sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: sukhāyavedanāya-
 sampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ
 kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 nakusalo ca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto nakusalo ca dhammā
 uppajjanti hetupaccayā: sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ
 paṭicca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto nakusalo ca nādukkhamasukhāya-
 vedanāyasampayutto nakusalo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:
 sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 nakusalo ca nadukkhāyavedanāyasampayutto nakusalo ca
 dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: sukhāyavedanāyasampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca nasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto nakusalo ca nadukkhāya-
 vedanāyasampayutto nakusalo ca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāyasampayutto
 nakusalo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā: satta. Adukkhamasukhāya-
 vedanāyasampayuttaṃ kusalaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nādukkhamasukhāyavedanāya-
 sampayutto nakusalo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: satta.

      [1024] Hetuṃ dassanenapahātabbahetukaṃ ... Tīṇiyeva.
      [1025] Nahetuṃ nadassanenapahātabbahetukaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu
 nanadassanenapahātabbahetuko dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
      [1026] Hetuyā ekaṃ.
                          .pe.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 334]
                Hetudukasaraṇaduke nahetudukanasaraṇadukaṃ
      [1027] Hetuṃ saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nasaraṇo dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ saraṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nanahetu
 nasaraṇo dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: hetuṃ saraṇañca nahetuṃ
 saraṇañca dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetu nasaraṇo dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā:
      [1028] Hetuyā tīṇi.
      [1029] Nahetuṃ araṇaṃ dhammaṃ paccayā nanahetu nāraṇo dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā:.
      [1030] Nahetuṃ araṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca 1- nanahetu nāraṇo dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: nahetuṃ araṇaṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca 2- nahetu
 nāraṇo ca nanahetu nāraṇo ca dhammā uppajjanti hetupaccayā:.
      [1031] Hetuyā tīṇi.
                     --------------
              Sahetukadukahetuduke nasahetukadukanahetudukaṃ
      [1032] Sahetukaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nasahetuko nahetu dhammo
 uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi. Ahetukaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nāhetuko nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [1033] Hetuyā cha.
      [1034] Sahetukaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nāhetuko nanahetu

  Footnote:1-2 Ma. paccayā.

[page 335]
 Dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā.
      [1035] Hetuyā tīṇi.
          Hetusampayuttadukahetuduke nahetusampayuttadukanahetudukaṃ

 hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nahetuvippayutto nahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: tīṇi.
      [1037] Hetuyā cha.

 nanahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.

 hetupaccayā: tīṇi.

              Chagocchakadukahetuduke chagocchakadukanahetudukaṃ
      [1094] Saññojanaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nosaññojano

 nogantho nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: oghaṃ hetuṃ
 dhammaṃ paṭicca noogho nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:
 yogaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca noyogo nahetu dhammo uppajjati
 hetupaccayā: nīvaraṇaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nonīvaraṇo nahetu
 dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: noparāmāsaṃ hetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca
 nanoparāmāso nahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā:.

      [1150] Saraṇaṃ nahetuṃ dhammaṃ paṭicca nāraṇo nanahetu dhammo

 nasaraṇo nanahetu dhammo uppajjati hetupaccayā: ekaṃ.
